- **review-agent-failed-to-apply-its-ignor-ecafde6175b90bf0**: Fix for review agents that post findings on vendored, generated, or third-party code despite an ignore list. Use when vendor or generated files get comments, the ignore config exists but findings still appear, or runs waste budget on files nobody owns. Key triggers: findings in vendor/, node_modules, or generated files; ignore config present but ineffective. Not for disagreements about whether a file should be ignored. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/review-agent-failed-to-apply-its-ignor-ecafde6175b90bf0))
- **review-agent-failed-to-cache-the-diff-d2ce9d512b2d2472**: Fixes a review agent re-fetching the PR diff on every retry by caching the diff keyed on PR number and head SHA, and reusing it across retries. Use when retries repeat expensive fetches. Triggers: duplicate diff fetches, retry budget burn. Not for first-fetch failures, or diffs that legitimately changed between attempts. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/review-agent-failed-to-cache-the-diff-d2ce9d512b2d2472))
- **review-agent-failed-to-calibrate-sever-8b82ef8c2e524a77**: Fix for review agents that label every finding critical, which trains reviewers to ignore the label entirely. Use when severity distributions skew to the top, trivial nits ship as critical, or reviewers complain everything is on fire. Key triggers: all-critical runs, style nits marked critical, severity ignored by reviewers. Not for PRs that genuinely contain multiple critical issues. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/review-agent-failed-to-calibrate-sever-8b82ef8c2e524a77))
- **review-agent-failed-to-catch-a-real-sq-62638df40f9b9a72**: Debugs a review agent missing a real SQL injection by reproducing the miss with a test case, finding the gap in its detection rules, and adding the missing pattern. Use when the reviewer overlooks a known-bad security construct in the diff. Triggers: missed injections, silent dangerous patterns. Not for false positives, or vulnerability classes outside the agent's checklist. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/review-agent-failed-to-catch-a-real-sq-62638df40f9b9a72))
- **review-agent-failed-to-check-resolved-2f3c65696d42005a**: Fix for review agents that open a new review thread on a topic a human already discussed and resolved. Use when the bot re-raises settled threads, annoys reviewers with repeats, or posts beside an existing conversation instead of replying to it. Key triggers: duplicate threads on the same lines, resolved threads re-litigated, parallel conversations on one issue. Not for duplicate findings within a single review run. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/review-agent-failed-to-check-resolved-2f3c65696d42005a))
- **review-agent-failed-to-dedupe-double-p-a274124fa87506f6**: Fixes a review agent posting duplicate summary comments on retry by checking for its existing comment before posting, and updating in place instead. Use when retries or re-runs produce duplicate bot summaries on a PR. Triggers: double summaries after retry, re-run duplicates. Not for duplicate inline review threads, human-posted duplicates, or comments the bot intentionally posts per commit. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/review-agent-failed-to-dedupe-double-p-a274124fa87506f6))
- **review-agent-failed-to-detect-a-draft-b89303d96602e9bc**: Fix for review agents that request changes on draft PRs, which cannot be merged anyway and where early verdicts just create noise. Use when the bot blocks or nags on drafts, reviewers get change requests on work-in-progress code, or draft authors complain about premature reviews. Key triggers: draft PRs receiving formal verdicts. Not for informational comments on drafts, which are fine. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/review-agent-failed-to-detect-a-draft-b89303d96602e9bc))
- **review-agent-failed-to-distinguish-tes-a61654a5d9f02b52**: Fix for review agents that rate test-code issues with production severity, or let real bugs hide because test and prod findings blur together. Use when test nits ship as high severity, prod bugs hide in a flood of test findings, or reviewers cannot tell which findings matter for the release. Key triggers: test paths in findings, severity complaints, mixed test/prod finding lists. Not for repos where tests ship to production. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/review-agent-failed-to-distinguish-tes-a61654a5d9f02b52))
- **review-agent-failed-to-filter-false-po-73bc52136f11662a**: Fixes a review agent flooding PRs with vulnerability findings on test fixtures by scoping security rules to production code paths. Use when fixtures, fakes, and mocks trigger injection or hardcoded-credential rules. Triggers: fixture findings, test-path noise. Not for real credentials committed in tests, or genuine findings in production code. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/review-agent-failed-to-filter-false-po-73bc52136f11662a))
